Badianus Manuscript, 1552
✦   ✦   ✦

A Catalogue of Medicinal Plants

Plantacopia

a whole plant catalogue
five thousand years in the making

34 traditions · 2,836 passages · 12 civilizations

wholeplantcatalog.com

— ❧ —
Search 2,836 passages across 34 traditions · 5,000 years
◆ psychedelics ◆ long life ◆ fever / malaria ◆ anxiety ◆ wound healing ◆ ginger & turmeric ◆ joint pain ◆ digestion

In 1971, a Chinese pharmacologist read a 4th-century Daoist text, found a reference to sweet wormwood for fever, isolated artemisinin, and won the Nobel Prize. The answers to medicine's most urgent questions are sitting in these books.

Featured Inquiries

Consciousness & Healing
Psychedelics Across Traditions
Ayahuasca, peyote, psilocybin mushrooms, and ritual plant medicines documented from Amazonian, Mesoamerican, North American, and Tibetan traditions.
Longevity & Vitality
Regimens for Long Life
Rasayana from Ayurveda, Jing tonics from Chinese medicine, Tibetan rejuvenation formulas, and WHO-validated adaptogens. Five thousand years of anti-aging knowledge.
Infectious Disease
Fever, Malaria & Wound Healing
From artemisinin in Daoist texts to cinchona bark in Peru to Egyptian wound dressings. The plants that changed medicine documented in their original sources.
Convergence
Plants in Every Tradition
Ginger, turmeric, garlic, and onion appear in nearly every tradition from Egypt to Japan. See how 23 cultures documented the same plants across 4,000 years.

Badianus Manuscript, 1552
✦   ✦   ✦

A Catalogue of Medicinal Plants

Plantacopia

a whole plant catalogue
five thousand years in the making

34 traditions · 2,836 passages · 12 civilizations

wholeplantcatalog.com

— ❧ —
Search 2,836 passages across 34 traditions · 5,000 years
◆ psychedelics ◆ long life ◆ fever / malaria ◆ anxiety ◆ wound healing ◆ ginger & turmeric ◆ joint pain ◆ digestion

In 1971, a Chinese pharmacologist read a 4th-century Daoist text, found a reference to sweet wormwood for fever, isolated artemisinin, and won the Nobel Prize. The answers to medicine's most urgent questions are sitting in these books.

Featured Inquiries

Consciousness & Healing
Psychedelics Across Traditions
Ayahuasca, peyote, psilocybin mushrooms, and ritual plant medicines documented from Amazonian, Mesoamerican, North American, and Tibetan traditions.
Longevity & Vitality
Regimens for Long Life
Rasayana from Ayurveda, Jing tonics from Chinese medicine, Tibetan rejuvenation formulas, and WHO-validated adaptogens. Five thousand years of anti-aging knowledge.
Infectious Disease
Fever, Malaria & Wound Healing
From artemisinin in Daoist texts to cinchona bark in Peru to Egyptian wound dressings. The plants that changed medicine documented in their original sources.
Convergence
Plants in Every Tradition
Ginger, turmeric, garlic, and onion appear in nearly every tradition from Egypt to Japan. See how 23 cultures documented the same plants across 4,000 years.

Badianus Manuscript, 1552
✦   ✦   ✦

A Catalogue of Medicinal Plants

Plantacopia

a whole plant catalogue
five thousand years in the making

34 traditions · 2,836 passages · 12 civilizations

wholeplantcatalog.com

— ❧ —
Search 2,836 passages across 34 traditions · 5,000 years
◆ psychedelics ◆ long life ◆ fever / malaria ◆ anxiety ◆ wound healing ◆ ginger & turmeric ◆ joint pain ◆ digestion

In 1971, a Chinese pharmacologist read a 4th-century Daoist text, found a reference to sweet wormwood for fever, isolated artemisinin, and won the Nobel Prize. The answers to medicine's most urgent questions are sitting in these books.

Featured Inquiries

Consciousness & Healing
Psychedelics Across Traditions
Ayahuasca, peyote, psilocybin mushrooms, and ritual plant medicines documented from Amazonian, Mesoamerican, North American, and Tibetan traditions.
Longevity & Vitality
Regimens for Long Life
Rasayana from Ayurveda, Jing tonics from Chinese medicine, Tibetan rejuvenation formulas, and WHO-validated adaptogens. Five thousand years of anti-aging knowledge.
Infectious Disease
Fever, Malaria & Wound Healing
From artemisinin in Daoist texts to cinchona bark in Peru to Egyptian wound dressings. The plants that changed medicine documented in their original sources.
Convergence
Plants in Every Tradition
Ginger, turmeric, garlic, and onion appear in nearly every tradition from Egypt to Japan. See how 23 cultures documented the same plants across 4,000 years.

A Catalogue of Medicinal Plants

Plantacopia

a whole plant catalogue
five thousand years in the making

34 traditions · 2,836 passages · 12 civilizations

wholeplantcatalog.com

— ❧ —
Search 2,836 passages across 34 traditions · 5,000 years
◆ psychedelics ◆ long life ◆ fever / malaria ◆ anxiety ◆ wound healing ◆ ginger & turmeric ◆ joint pain ◆ digestion

In 1971, a Chinese pharmacologist read a 4th-century Daoist text, found a reference to sweet wormwood for fever, isolated artemisinin, and won the Nobel Prize. The answers to medicine's most urgent questions are sitting in these books.

Featured Inquiries

Consciousness & Healing
Psychedelics Across Traditions
Ayahuasca, peyote, psilocybin mushrooms, and ritual plant medicines documented from Amazonian, Mesoamerican, North American, and Tibetan traditions.
Longevity & Vitality
Regimens for Long Life
Rasayana from Ayurveda, Jing tonics from Chinese medicine, Tibetan rejuvenation formulas, and WHO-validated adaptogens. Five thousand years of anti-aging knowledge.
Infectious Disease
Fever, Malaria & Wound Healing
From artemisinin in Daoist texts to cinchona bark in Peru to Egyptian wound dressings. The plants that changed medicine documented in their original sources.
Convergence
Plants in Every Tradition
Ginger, turmeric, garlic, and onion appear in nearly every tradition from Egypt to Japan. See how 23 cultures documented the same plants across 4,000 years.

Modern Research

You are the scientist. Each card runs a real query across 2,844 passages from 34 traditions spanning 5,000 years. Tap any card to search — results appear in the Corpus tab.

◆ Convergence — when isolated civilizations reach the same answer

Independent Convergence
Same plant, same condition, zero cultural contact
When the Amazon rainforest, Classical China, and ancient Egypt all prescribe the same remedy — that is pharmacology, not folklore. Artemisinin began this way.
→ Query 34 traditions for matching plant + condition
Geographic Impossibility
Pre-contact knowledge across separated continents
Lost trade routes, parallel discovery, or ancient migrations hidden in botanical knowledge. What plants appear on both sides of the Atlantic before Columbus?
→ Cross-reference Americas vs. Asia vs. Africa vs. Europe

◆ Untapped Medicine — what pharmacology has not yet studied

Untapped by Modern Medicine
5+ traditions agree — no clinical trial exists
Identify plants with cross-traditional consensus and consistent dosage but zero FDA-approved equivalent. The pharmacological low-hanging fruit of ethnobotany.
→ Search multi-tradition consensus
Pre-Antibiotic Infection Control
5,000 years of fighting bacteria before penicillin
In the age of antibiotic resistance, the pre-1928 pharmacopoeia is newly urgent. Bald's Leechbook eye salve kills MRSA. What else in the corpus has antimicrobial potential?
→ Filter: infection, wound, fever, sepsis
Nervous System & Mental Health
Ancient anxiolytics, sedatives, antidepressants
Valerian, kava, ashwagandha, bacopa, saffron — some now in clinical trials. What else appeared for anxiety, grief, insomnia across traditions that psychiatry has ignored?
→ Search: anxiety, sleep, grief, calm, mind
Pain & Analgesia
Non-opioid pain management across 5,000 years
Willow bark became aspirin. The corpus holds hundreds of pain entries. Which non-opioid analgesics appear across traditions with no pharmaceutical equivalent?
→ Filter: pain, headache, inflammation, analgesia

◆ Longevity, Women's Health & Psychopharmacology

Longevity Consensus
Plants for long life endorsed by 3+ unrelated traditions
Ginseng, turmeric, ashwagandha — all appear in multiple longevity traditions. Do they share a mechanism like AMPK activation? What else carries this cross-cultural endorsement?
→ Query: longevity, vitality, tonic, rejuvenation
Women's Health
The most systematically understudied domain
Reproductive medicine forms the largest category in most traditional pharmacopoeias. Modern clinical research has studied almost none of it. The corpus disagrees with this omission.
→ Search: fertility, menstrual, childbirth, menopause
Plant Psychopharmacology
Ritual, vision, and consciousness — the pre-modern record
Peyote, ayahuasca, psilocybin, kava, blue lotus — each now in clinical trials. The corpus documents pre-modern dosage and framing. How does it compare to current protocols?
→ Search: vision, ritual, trance, consciousness

◆ Your Research Question

Badianus Manuscript, 1552
✦   ✦   ✦

A Catalogue of Medicinal Plants

Plantacopia

a whole plant catalogue
five thousand years in the making

34 traditions · 2,836 passages · 12 civilizations

wholeplantcatalog.com

— ❧ —
Search 2,836 passages across 34 traditions · 5,000 years
◆ psychedelics ◆ long life ◆ fever / malaria ◆ anxiety ◆ wound healing ◆ ginger & turmeric ◆ joint pain ◆ digestion

In 1971, a Chinese pharmacologist read a 4th-century Daoist text, found a reference to sweet wormwood for fever, isolated artemisinin, and won the Nobel Prize. The answers to medicine's most urgent questions are sitting in these books.

Featured Inquiries

Consciousness & Healing
Psychedelics Across Traditions
Ayahuasca, peyote, psilocybin mushrooms, and ritual plant medicines documented from Amazonian, Mesoamerican, North American, and Tibetan traditions.
Longevity & Vitality
Regimens for Long Life
Rasayana from Ayurveda, Jing tonics from Chinese medicine, Tibetan rejuvenation formulas, and WHO-validated adaptogens. Five thousand years of anti-aging knowledge.
Infectious Disease
Fever, Malaria & Wound Healing
From artemisinin in Daoist texts to cinchona bark in Peru to Egyptian wound dressings. The plants that changed medicine documented in their original sources.
Convergence
Plants in Every Tradition
Ginger, turmeric, garlic, and onion appear in nearly every tradition from Egypt to Japan. See how 23 cultures documented the same plants across 4,000 years.

Badianus Manuscript, 1552
✦   ✦   ✦

A Catalogue of Medicinal Plants

Plantacopia

a whole plant catalogue
five thousand years in the making

34 traditions · 2,836 passages · 12 civilizations

wholeplantcatalog.com

— ❧ —
Search 2,836 passages across 34 traditions · 5,000 years
◆ psychedelics ◆ long life ◆ fever / malaria ◆ anxiety ◆ wound healing ◆ ginger & turmeric ◆ joint pain ◆ digestion

In 1971, a Chinese pharmacologist read a 4th-century Daoist text, found a reference to sweet wormwood for fever, isolated artemisinin, and won the Nobel Prize. The answers to medicine's most urgent questions are sitting in these books.

Featured Inquiries

Consciousness & Healing
Psychedelics Across Traditions
Ayahuasca, peyote, psilocybin mushrooms, and ritual plant medicines documented from Amazonian, Mesoamerican, North American, and Tibetan traditions.
Longevity & Vitality
Regimens for Long Life
Rasayana from Ayurveda, Jing tonics from Chinese medicine, Tibetan rejuvenation formulas, and WHO-validated adaptogens. Five thousand years of anti-aging knowledge.
Infectious Disease
Fever, Malaria & Wound Healing
From artemisinin in Daoist texts to cinchona bark in Peru to Egyptian wound dressings. The plants that changed medicine documented in their original sources.
Convergence
Plants in Every Tradition
Ginger, turmeric, garlic, and onion appear in nearly every tradition from Egypt to Japan. See how 23 cultures documented the same plants across 4,000 years.

A Catalogue of Medicinal Plants

Plantacopia

a whole plant catalogue
five thousand years in the making

34 traditions · 2,836 passages · 12 civilizations

Search 2,836 passages across 34 traditions · 5,000 years
◆ psychedelics ◆ long life ◆ fever / malaria ◆ anxiety ◆ wound healing ◆ ginger & turmeric ◆ joint pain ◆ digestion

In 1971, a Chinese pharmacologist read a 4th-century Daoist text, found a reference to sweet wormwood for fever, isolated artemisinin, and won the Nobel Prize. The answers to medicine's most urgent questions are sitting in these books.

Featured Inquiries

Consciousness & Healing
Psychedelics Across Traditions
Ayahuasca, peyote, psilocybin mushrooms, and ritual plant medicines documented from Amazonian, Mesoamerican, North American, and Tibetan traditions.
Longevity & Vitality
Regimens for Long Life
Rasayana from Ayurveda, Jing tonics from Chinese medicine, Tibetan rejuvenation formulas, and WHO-validated adaptogens. Five thousand years of anti-aging knowledge.
Infectious Disease
Fever, Malaria & Wound Healing
From artemisinin in Daoist texts to cinchona bark in Peru to Egyptian wound dressings. The plants that changed medicine documented in their original sources.
Convergence
Plants in Every Tradition
Ginger, turmeric, garlic, and onion appear in nearly every tradition from Egypt to Japan. See how 23 cultures documented the same plants across 4,000 years.
